- [x] **Coaches as a first-class entity + coach-above-expectation metric** *(shipped — PR1 ingest + PR2 CAE compute + PR3 surfacing layer, all 2026-05-31; design doc `docs/coach_above_expectation_design.md`. Editorial coaching-change retrospectives (surface d) remain a deferred follow-on.)* — once a coach→team→season mapping from `coachdict.json` lands, the same data unlocks a coaching-analysis surface. **Core metric**: *coach value above roster expectation* = actual team AdjEM − roster-projected AdjEM, aggregated over a coach's multi-year tenure. **Feasibility measured: GREEN for a descriptive metric, but only against the roster-talent-only projection (`phase_b`), NOT the served blend** — `served = 0.5·baseline + 0.5·phase_b` has a baseline=prior-AdjEM term that absorbs the persistent coach signal, driving σ²_between to 0 (this is why the PR E de-risk's `served`-based null looked so dead). Against `phase_b`: σ²_between σ≈2.9 AdjEM, ICC 0.135, persistence weak-positive (moved-teams +0.11), and the top list is face-valid mid-major-heavy (Randy Bennett/St. Mary's, Schertz, DeVries, Sendek, Dusty May). **Key design constraints** (see doc): denominator must be `phase_b`; per-quartile de-bias the projection first (else CAE inherits the Q1/Q4 tail bias as fake coach credit); heavy EB shrinkage (k≈6.4 season-equivalents, reliability 0.24→0.44 over 2→5 seasons); coach/program confound is unresolvable at 5-season depth (frame as coach×program over-expectation); name dedup is a real gotcha (Rick Pitino ≠ Richard Pitino, both present). **Descriptive-only for v1** — the predictive-feature use is separately gated and currently fails the lift bar (moved-team signal too thin out-of-sample). Phasing: **PR1 — coachdict ingest + entity model: SHIPPED 2026-05-31.** Migration `024_coaches.sql` (`coaches` entity + `coach_seasons` mapping); `TorkvikClient::fetch_coachdict` + `ingest/coaches.rs` + `cstat-ingest coaches [--year]`. Ingested 12 seasons (2015–2026): 4,294 coach-seasons, **99.4% team match** (4,267/4,294 — the 27 NULLs are all genuinely-absent team-seasons: 2021 COVID Ivies, dropped-D-I Hartford/St. Francis NY, Le Moyne gap, a coachdict typo, the Houston Baptist→Christian rename), 735 distinct coaches, **657 `is_new_hc` flags** (the PR E new-coach signal, delivered for free). Two data-quality fixes landed: (a) coachdict carries redundant **inverted entries** (`"Matt McMahon"→"LSU"` alongside `"LSU"→"Matt McMahon"`) that would mint junk coach entities named after teams — filtered precisely (mirror exists + value-is-team + key-isn't, so legit unmatched teams survive); (b) three coachdict-spelling aliases added to the shared `team_name_match` (Texas A&M Corpus Christi w/ both hyphen variants, UT Martin, Arkansas Little Rock). Rick Pitino ≠ Richard Pitino verified distinct. Unit tests cover the change-flag, inversion filter, and name-identity. coachdict endpoint documented in `docs/torvik-api-guide.md` §4. **PR2 — CAE compute: SHIPPED 2026-05-31.** Migration `025_coach_ratings.sql` (`coach_season_cae` per-team-season + `coach_ratings` career), offline job `training/compute_cae.py` (`--write` gates on the guards). Joined 1,326 team-seasons (100% of the 5-season backtest, via the `teams.natstat_id` hop since the dump records the base-season UUID) → **491 coach ratings** (265 with ≥3 seasons). **Build decision: ship RAW as the headline, not the de-biased value.** De-bias was planned by *actual* quartile but that bakes in outcome-conditioned regression ([[project_projection_q1_bias_refuted]]); switched to *projection* (phase_b) quartile, which is artifact-free and found the projection is miscalibrated only at its low end (Q1/Q2 ≈ −1.7, no Q4 under-projection — so the feared blue-blood inflation never appears). Empirically the de-bias strips the *program* component (same-team persistence +0.047→−0.009; moved-teams transferable +0.112→+0.083 preserved) and drops overall split-half below significance (+0.114 z≈2.1 → +0.049 z≈0.9). So RAW = "coach×program over-expectation" (the design's framing, the only significant-persistence variant) is the leaderboard value; the projection-quartile-de-biased value is stored alongside (`cae_adj_*`) as the conservative prestige-adjusted lower bound. Guards pass on raw (ICC 0.135, split-half +0.114). Top-15 face-valid (Schertz, R. Pitino, DeVries, Sendek, Calhoun, Golden, Willard, Collins — mid-major overachievers + elite developers, no blue-blood dominance; bottom is struggling low-majors). → **PR3 — surfacing layer: SHIPPED 2026-05-31.** Pure read-path: **no migration, no compute** (PR1/PR2 froze everything into `coaches`/`coach_seasons`/`coach_season_cae`/`coach_ratings`); all new query fns live in `queries.rs`, routes in `crates/cstat-api/src/routes/coaches.rs`. Three routes, all independent of the slow `team_detail` projection loop: **(1)** `GET /api/coaches` leaderboard over `coach_ratings`, default-sort `cae_shrunk DESC`. **(2)** `GET /api/coaches/{id}` detail — `coach_ratings` + `coach_season_cae` (per-season residual sparkline; `actual_adjem`/`projection` are stored columns, so **zero live inference**). **(3)** `GET /api/teams/{id}/coach` — **dedicated route, deliberately NOT folded into `/api/teams/{id}`** (that handler is latency-bound by its ~30-game serial `predict_projection` loop, `teams.rs:132`; a separate route lets the card render immediately and stays decoupled from the projection-loop optimization in Refactor Backlog). Frontend: `web/src/pages/Coaches.tsx` (leaderboard) + `CoachDetail.tsx` (SVG sparkline) + shared `components/cae.ts` (fmt/color helpers); coach card on TeamDetail via a second decoupled fetch; routes in `App.tsx`, nav in `Layout.tsx` (desktop + mobile). **Display contract held** (migration 025): headline `cae_shrunk` (raw EB-shrunk), never `cae_raw_mean`; credibility band (`ci_low/high` + `reliability`) always shown; `cae_adj_shrunk` secondary; copy is descriptive "coach×program over-expectation," not predictive. **Three decisions emerged during build:** **(a) Season scoping + Career/Season toggle** — the navbar season picker was inert against a season-independent career rating, so: *career mode* now filters the list to coaches active in the picked season (rating unchanged; team column + deep-link reflect that season), and a new *season mode* (`?mode=season`, `get_coach_season_leaderboard`) re-ranks by the selected year's single-season `cae_raw` — noisier, framed as a "who overachieved this year" snapshot, and it surfaces the roadmap's own anchor cluster (Bruce Pearl/Auburn +22.8, Gates/Missouri +17.4 in 2025). Picker is constrained to CAE coverage (2022–2026) via an `available_seasons` field; an out-of-coverage `?season=` snaps to the newest covered year. **(b) Dropped the `min_seasons` UI filter** — `cae_shrunk` shrinkage already pulls thin tenures toward 0 and the reliability column conveys confidence, so the ≥1/≥3/≥5 buttons were redundant clutter; the career board now shows all 491 rated coaches (backend param retained for API flexibility, frontend requests 1). **(c) Fixed a `coach_seasons` fan-out bug** — coachdict carries redundant team-name *variants* for 9 (coach, season) pairs (e.g. `Tennessee Martin`+`UT Martin`, `Saint Joseph's`+`St. Joseph's`, `Maryland Eastern Shore(s)`, plus a stale `Hartford` for John Gallagher after Hartford left D-I), so a direct `coach_seasons` join doubled those coaches — one row with the matched team, one with a NULL "—" team. Fixed read-path via `LEFT JOIN LATERAL … ORDER BY (team_id IS NOT NULL) DESC LIMIT 1` dedup in the season-leaderboard + coach-detail queries (the career-leaderboard lateral already filtered NULL teams). **Ingest-side follow-up (low priority, read path now correct):** the cleaner fix is to not mint duplicate `coach_seasons` rows at ingest — collapse name variants by resolved `team_natstat_id`, prefer the matched row (`UNIQUE (season, coachdict_team_name)` in migration 024 permits the dupes today; an ingest dedup or a pruning migration would close it). **Out of scope for PR3 (deferred):** surface (c) new-coach tint on the Future/projections tab (folds into "(4) Projections UI regression caveat") and surface (d) coaching-change retrospectives (editorial, separate PR) — the lightweight "New HC" badge on the TeamDetail card + season-mode board shipped since `is_new_hc` is free. Biggest lever to upgrade from "fun grade" to "trustworthy rating": more seasons of roster projections (extend the #96 backtest pre-2022), blocked on pre-2021 transfer quality. We already have the exact denominator — the preseason **roster projection engine** (`cstat_core::roster_projection::score_projection_adj_em`, the served `/api/projections` number) gives each team-season a roster-derived expected AdjEM independent of who coaches it; the residual is a clean "did the coach over/under-perform the talent on hand" signal, and averaging it across a coach's seasons separates persistent coaching skill from single-season noise. This is the coaching analog of the player CamPom residual. **Why it's honest-ish**: the projection is roster-talent-only (it does *not* see coaching/scheme), so the residual genuinely isolates the non-roster component — with the usual caveat that it also absorbs injuries, scheme luck, and SOS error, so multi-year aggregation + shrinkage toward zero is load-bearing (a 1-season +15 residual is mostly noise; a 5-season +4 mean is signal). **Possible surfaces**: (a) a `/coaches` leaderboard ranked by career coach-above-expectation, with tenure, team history, and trajectory sparkline; (b) coach cards on TeamDetail ("Jon Scheyer: +2.1 AdjEM above roster expectation over 3 seasons"); (c) a "new coach" tint on the projections/Future tab tied to the PR E flag; (d) coaching-change retrospectives (Auburn pre/post Bruce→Steven Pearl, Missouri's Gates reversal). **Data**: coachdict goes back to 1893 but our roster projections only exist for ingested seasons (2015→2026 today, 2007+ possible), so the metric is bounded by projection coverage, not coach coverage. **Decoupled from PR E**: PR E only needs the boolean change-flag; this needs the full coach-name mapping + a new `coaches` table (or a `coach` column on `team_season_stats`) + aggregation queries + API + frontend. Multi-PR; design-doc-worthy. Sequence after PR E proves the coachdict join is clean.
- [x] **Coach rankings: add team-strength columns + a blended "overall impact" ranking (display-only, MUST NOT feed projections)** *(proposed 2026-05-31; **SHIPPED 2026-06-01**)* — enrich the `/coaches` leaderboard + coach detail with the coach's **actual team AdjEM, AdjO, AdjD** (per-season + career mean), and add an optional **blended ranking** combining raw team strength with CAE, so a coach who has a strong team *and* squeezes extra out of it outranks one who merely meets a weak roster. **Motivation**: CAE alone is "value above roster," which by design parks elite-roster coaches near 0 (see the "great roster" caveat on the projections item below) and tops the board with overachieving underdogs; a blend answers the complementary question — "who's actually delivering strong, tough-schedule teams." **SOS comes for free**: AdjEM is already opponent-adjusted, so ranking on it (or a blend) inherently rewards hard schedules — no separate SOS term needed. **Blend design (TBD)**: e.g. `z(CAE) + z(career AdjEM)` as an alternate sort, or a documented weighting; label it explicitly as an *evaluative* composite ("results + overperformance"), not a rigorous metric — raw AdjEM carries roster quality + variance, so the blend is a lens, not a truth. **Hard architectural boundary — these columns are display-only and must NEVER round-trip into projections.** Raw team AdjEM *is* the projection target; feeding it (or any blend containing it) back into the forecast is direct target leakage / dogfooding, and even a coach's prior-season raw AdjEM double-counts roster quality the projection already models. The clean split: **projections may use only the orthogonal, PIT, shrunk CAE residual** (next item); **rankings may show raw strength + blends** for human evaluation. CAE = forecast-safe coach signal; AdjEM/blend = descriptive-only. Keep the wall explicit in code — the projection path never imports the raw-strength columns. **Effort**: near-pure read-path — `team_season_stats` already carries AdjEM/AdjO/AdjD per (team, season); join via `coach_seasons`, add the blend formula. No new compute. **SHIPPED 2026-06-01 — pure read-path, no migration, no compute, no model change.** All new SQL in `queries.rs`; handler wiring in `routes/coaches.rs`; frontend in `Coaches.tsx` / `CoachDetail.tsx` / `client.ts`. **Strength columns**: career-mean **AdjEM/AdjO/AdjD** added to the career leaderboard + a "Career team strength" card block on coach detail; per-season **AdjO/AdjD** added to the detail season table and the single-season board (AdjEM was already stored as `actual_adjem`). Means come from a `LEFT JOIN LATERAL` aggregate over `coach_season_cae` → `teams (natstat_id, season)` → `team_season_stats (team_id, season)` — all unique-key 1:1 joins, no fan-out (verified). **Blend** = `z(cae_shrunk) + z(career_adj_em)` (career) / `z(cae_raw) + z(actual_adjem)` (season), computed in Rust post-fetch (`apply_blend`/`apply_career_blend`/`apply_season_blend` in `queries.rs`, `#[sqlx(default)]` so no backing column), z-scored over the qualified board with population std; rows missing AdjEM contribute only the CAE term, degenerate boards leave `blend` null. **Blend is the default sort** on both boards (per product call); CAE remains the headline metric and every column is sortable. **Truncation fix**: the frontend now requests `limit=500` (the API cap, > the ~360 D-I coaches in any season-scoped board) so the blend z-score population and the default sort see every qualified coach, not the old top-200-by-CAE slice. **The no-leakage wall is enforced and documented in every layer** (struct docs, SQL comments, TS types, UI tooltips) — adversarial review confirmed none of `career_adj_em/o/d`, `blend`, or the per-season `adj_offense/adj_defense` appear in `features.rs`/`inference.rs`/`roster_projection.rs`/`compute_projections.rs`; the projection path never imports them. **Also**: CI range divider `…`→`–` across the three coach surfaces (leaderboard, detail card, TeamDetail card); 5 unit tests cover the blend math (population z-scores, missing-AdjEM fallback, degenerate populations, season always-present-AdjEM path). **Known LOW gap (not frontend-reachable)**: the API-only no-season (`GET /api/coaches` with no `?season=`) career board has 688 qualifying coaches > the 500 cap, so its blend would be z-scored over a top-N-by-CAE slice; documented as an invariant on `apply_career_blend` (the caller must pass the full qualified population, which the always-season-scoped frontend does). **Deliberately deferred — coach +/- in projections**: the separate, leakage-gated item below (the additive `roster_projection + shrunk_coach_CAE` term) stays out of this PR — it crosses the display-only wall and needs the point-in-time CAE compute + a true PIT backtest first.
- [x] **Coach +/- in projections — additive coach term on the Future tab + served rankings (leakage-gated)** *(resolved 2026-06-03 — display column SHIPPED, served term REFUTED on the program-persistence null; the served forecast stays roster-only by design)* — surface a **coach +/- column** on the Future/projections pages and **factor the shrunk coach CAE into the projected AdjEM and the predicted rankings**, as an explicit, maintained decomposition: `forecast_adjem = roster_projection (coach-blind) + shrunk_coach_CAE`. **RESULT (2026-06-03):** built the PIT backtest (`training/pit_cae_backtest.py`) the validation plan below specified — walk-forward over targets 2019–2026, each coach's term EB-shrunk from seasons strictly `< Y`, scored `phase_b + term` vs `phase_b` alone. The coach term **clears the absolute floor** (overall MAE lift **+0.131**, z≈5.5; applied-subset |term|≥1 lift +0.34) — but it **FAILS the program-persistence null**: re-running the identical backtest keyed on **team** instead of coach gets a *bigger* lift (**+0.183**, z≈7.4), so `coach − program = −0.052`. The lift is **program-level projection-bias correction, not coaching** — exactly the coach×program confound the design flagged, now empirically refuted (the moved-team cut, the only place coach identity separates from program, is just z≈2.2 over n=69, consistent with the twice-refuted "moved-team signal too thin"). **Decision: ship the coach +/- DISPLAY-ONLY** (the planned fallback) and leave the served forecast roster-only. **SHIPPED 2026-06-03 — pure read-path on the Future tab, no migration, no compute, no model change:** display-only coach fields on `ProjectedTeam` (`crates/cstat-api/src/routes/projections.rs` — `fetch_coach_cae` resolves the coach via `coach_seasons` preferring the target season, else the base season; joins career `coach_ratings`), a "Coach +/-" column on `web/src/pages/Projected.tsx` (CAE color + reliability-as-opacity + link to coach detail) with a tooltip stating it is NOT in the projection. Hard wall held: the projection math (`shrink`/`predict_team`) never reads the coach fields; documented in struct/TS doc. The actually-useful +0.18 finding is split out as its own item below ([[project_pit_cae_program_null]]). *Original spec retained below for the served-term design, now parked:* surface a coach +/- column and factor the shrunk coach CAE into the projected AdjEM and rankings as an explicit decomposition: `forecast_adjem = roster_projection (coach-blind) + shrunk_coach_CAE`. **Why it's not circular**: the roster projection (`cstat_core::roster_projection::score_projection_adj_em`, the served `/api/projections` number) never sees coaching/scheme, and CAE is *defined* as `actual − roster_projection`; the two terms are orthogonal by construction, so summing them doesn't double-count. Store + display both so the breakdown is legible ("Duke: +28 roster, +2 Scheyer → +30"). **THE load-bearing gate — temporal leakage**: the coach term used for season Y must be computed from seasons strictly **< Y**. The stored `coach_ratings.cae_shrunk` aggregates all of 2022–2026, so it is point-in-time-valid *only* for the live 2027 forecast (all prior); **any accuracy backtest needs a point-in-time CAE recomputed per target year from prior seasons only**, mirroring the #96 LOSO roster backtest. Without PIT CAE the measured lift is fake — the same outcome-conditioned trap that killed PR B ([[project_projection_q1_bias_refuted]]). **Overfitting guards**: keep the heavy EB shrinkage (k≈6.4) so a 1–2 season coach contributes ~0; new/moved coaches → thin CAE → ~0 bonus (correct: unknown), tying into the PR E new-coach uncertainty band. **Honesty / prior art**: the *predictive-feature* use of CAE was refuted twice as an ML feature ("moved-team signal too thin out-of-sample"); this is a different framing (an explicit additive prior, not a learned feature), but it must STILL clear the lift bar on a true PIT backtest before the coach term moves the served number. **Fallback if it doesn't clear the bar**: ship the coach +/- as a *display* column / decomposition and leave the served forecast roster-only — the column is honest regardless of lift. **Validation plan**: (1) add a point-in-time mode to `training/compute_cae.py` (recompute coach CAE per target season, excluding that season and all later); (2) backtest `roster_proj + pit_coach_CAE` vs `roster_proj` alone on 2022–2026, MAE/RMSE on realized AdjEM, gate on lift beyond the established noise floor (~0.002 MAE, the level that killed the coaching-change indicator); (3) LOSO-only, watching moved-team and elite-roster coaches specifically. **Structural caveat — the "great roster" question**: CAE measures *value above the roster*, not roster construction. An elite recruiter-coach who exactly meets a +28 projection scores ~0 — **neutral, not penalized** — because their recruiting/portal/development wins are already banked in the roster projection. Combined with AdjEM ceiling compression (a +30 roster has far less headroom above its projection than a −15 roster has), the raw metric structurally favors overachieving underdogs at the top of the board and parks blue-blood coaches near 0. That's *correct* for what CAE measures (in-season coaching/development beyond expectation) but means the additive coach term mostly nudges the forecast at the margins and for clear over/under-performers — it must NOT re-credit blue bloods for talent the roster projection already counts. The projection is calibrated (verified in PR2: no systematic Q4 blue-blood under-projection; the low end IS under-projected, which is what inflates the underdog tail — the `cae_adj_*` projection-quartile de-bias is the conservative lever if that tail needs damping in the forecast). **Surfaces**: Future tab coach +/- column + decomposition tooltip; projected ranking sorts on roster+coach. **Predictions (`/predict`, game-level) — secondary/optional**: the margin model is a separate 49-feature ML model (`features.rs`); injecting a coach term there is a bigger, separately-gated change — follow-on only if the projection-side term validates. **Interaction with the preseason×pit blend** (commit e2233b9): the Future tab already decays the roster anchor toward point-in-time as games accrue; decide whether the coach term decays with it or stays a constant prior (leaning constant — coaching is a season-long prior, not something the in-season sample replaces — but confirm in the backtest). **Depends on**: PR1–PR3 (done) + the PIT-CAE compute. Design-doc-worthy if it goes past display-only.
- [x] **Program-persistence projection calibration — a team-keyed PIT residual prior on the served forecast: REFUTED 2026-06-03 (the served baseline blend already absorbs it)** *(proposed 2026-06-03; spun out of the PIT-CAE backtest, [[project_pit_cae_program_null]]; **REFUTED before build — measured the load-bearing question first**)* — **RESULT: do NOT wire a program prior into the served forecast.** The +0.18 MAE lift was measured against **raw `phase_b`**, but the served `/api/projections` number is `served = 0.50·baseline + 0.50·phase_b` and **baseline (= last season's actual AdjEM) is itself a per-program persistence term**. Built `training/pit_program_calibration.py` to test the prior against `served` directly (same walk-forward PIT, prior keyed on `team_natstat_id`, residual `actual − served`): the between-program variance **collapses from σ²_b=7.40 (σ=2.72, ICC=0.13) against `phase_b` to exactly σ²_b=0.000 against `served`** — the PIT prior is identically 0 on every team, lift +0.0000. This is the *same* trap the feasibility study flagged for the served-CAE null ("baseline absorbs the persistent signal, driving σ²_between to 0"). Worse than break-even, even: `served` MAE **5.78** already beats `phase_b + program_prior` (**5.86**), which beats `phase_b` alone (6.04) — so the existing 50%-baseline blend is a *stronger* use of program persistence than an explicit prior. **No served-forecast change, and no display-only decomposition either** (against `served` the program term is 0, so there is nothing legible to surface beyond the existing "55% last year + 45% roster" tooltip / "Δ vs last" column). Evidence: `training/pit_program_calibration.py` + `eval_history/pit_program_calibration_served_20260603_summary.json`. **Lesson banked**: any future "add a persistence prior to the projection" idea is pre-empted by the baseline term — to beat it you'd need a residual the baseline blend does *not* already capture (e.g. an O/D-split or schedule-structure signal), not a net-AdjEM program mean. *(Original spec, now parked:)* — the PIT-CAE backtest's real finding: a point-in-time prior that corrects each **program's** persistent roster-projection residual improves the served projection by **+0.18 MAE** (z≈7.4 over targets 2019–2026), *beating* the coach-keyed term (+0.13) — i.e. the lift is program-level, not coaching. **This is a projection-engine calibration item, NOT a coach feature** — keep it off the coaches surface entirely. **Mechanism**: for target season Y, compute each program's EB-shrunk mean of prior `(actual_AdjEM − phase_b)` residuals over seasons strictly **< Y**, then `forecast_adjem = phase_b + program_prior`. The prior captures stable per-program effects the roster projection is blind to (scheme/system continuity, home-venue strength, conference-strength or recruiting-rank systematic error, program infrastructure). Heavy EB shrinkage (re-estimate `σ²_w/σ²_b` on the `<Y` cohort each year, exactly as the coach term does) means a young/relocated program with little history contributes ≈0 — correct. **Honest framing**: this is a *bias correction on a coach-blind projector*, so it does NOT leak the coach signal and does NOT double-count roster talent (the residual is orthogonal to `phase_b` by construction). It is, however, an **autoregressive program prior** — it will be slow to react to a genuine program step-change (new coach, conference move, NIL surge); pair it with the existing preseason×pit blend (commit e2233b9) so in-season games override it as they accrue, and consider down-weighting it the year after a coaching change (tie to the `is_new_hc` flag). **THE leakage gate is identical to the coach term**: the prior for Y must use only seasons `< Y`; the stored value is PIT-valid only for the live forecast, and any accuracy claim needs the walk-forward backtest (already built — `training/pit_cae_backtest.py`, run it with `key='team_natstat_id'`). **Validation plan**: (1) confirm the +0.18 LOSO lift replicates on the production projection path (the backtest uses the dump's `phase_b`; wire the prior into `crates/cstat-api/src/routes/projections.rs::shrink` / the projection compose and re-measure end-to-end); (2) check it does NOT degrade the calibrated tails (the projection under-projects its low end — verify the prior doesn't over-correct mid-majors into the top); (3) decide storage — a new `team_projection_prior (season, team_natstat_id, prior, reliability)` table populated by a PIT offline job mirroring `compute_cae.py`, or compute it inline in the projection route from `team_season_stats` + the backtest dump. **Surfaces**: a decomposition on the Future tab (`Houston: +32 roster, +1.4 program → +33`), parallel to the now-display-only coach column; the served `/api/projections` midpoint shifts to `phase_b + program_prior`. **Effort**: Python PIT compute (small, the backtest already does it) + Rust wire-up into the served projection (medium — it moves the headline number, so it re-clears the projection accuracy bar) + a migration if stored. Design-doc-worthy since it changes the served forecast. **Sequence**: independent of the O/D splits below; this is the highest-leverage projection-accuracy lever currently on the board.
- [x] **Turnover-conditional projection blend — lean off the stale baseline for roster-overhaul teams** *(proposed + **SHIPPED 2026-06-03**; the survivor of the program-persistence refutation above)* — the program-persistence refutation banked a lesson: to beat `served` you need a signal the 50%-baseline term does *not* already capture. Roster turnover is exactly that. `baseline` (last season's AdjEM) is a **stale anchor** when a team changes coaches or overhauls its roster — and baseline can't encode its own reliability, so a blend weight that *leans off baseline* for transition teams uses information orthogonal to baseline's value (the one place there's room). **Diagnostic** (`training/transition_blend_diagnostic.py`, 2019–2026, n=2431): new-HC teams are over-projected by **+1.08 AdjEM** with served(.5) MAE 6.79 (*worse* than talent-only 6.75 — the 50% baseline weight actively hurts them), optimal weight 0.20; overhaul teams (<40% talent retained) bias +0.71, optimal w=0.30; continuity teams optimal w=0.50 (flat is right). **Turnover beat is_new_hc** as the trigger (LOSO lift +0.035 vs +0.029) — it directly measures baseline staleness, subsumes 61% of new-HC teams, has no false positives from same-roster coaching changes, and lives on `ProjectedRoster` (no DB fetch → both serving surfaces stay in lockstep). **SHIPPED**: `w` ramps from the stable **0.50** (retained ≥0.40) to **0.25** (retained ≤0.20), keyed on `retained_talent_fraction = Σ cam_v3 returners / (returners + departures)`. All in the shared `roster_projection.rs` (`PROJECTION_SHRINK_WEIGHT_OVERHAUL`, `transition_shrink_weight`, `retained_talent_fraction`, `shrink_adj_em_weighted`) so the `/api/projections` route (`predict_team`) and the offline `score_projection_adj_em` (→ persisted `team_preseason_projection` → predict route's preseason×pit blend) derive the same weight from the same roster. LOSO-validated **+0.04 MAE pooled** (~+0.20 on new-coach teams) and it corrects the overhaul over-projection bias. Surfaced on `ProjectedTeam.baseline_weight` + a Future-tab tooltip / ⟳ marker for roster-leaning teams. Smoke: 95/364 teams down-weighted for 2027 (Arkansas, Louisville, Tennessee, Texas, Kansas… — recognizable heavy-portal programs), 269 unchanged at 0.50. 8 new unit tests; clippy/fmt/tsc/eslint/vite clean. Methodology: `docs/projections_methodology.md` *Scoring & calibration*. **Not done (deferred)**: a coaching-change *event study* using `is_new_hc` as a natural experiment to separate coach-vs-program AdjEM impact (the +1.08 new-HC over-projection is already suggestive — a new coach is, on average, a ~1-AdjEM step down from the program+old-coach expectation).
- [x] **Offensive / Defensive CAE splits (CAE-O / CAE-D) — REFUTED 2026-06-05** *(de-risked before any model work; scripts `training/validation/derisk_cae_od_{signal,school_changers,power}.py`, memory [[project_campom_od_primitives_finding]])*. The split was killed by the **coach-vs-program travel test**: offensive/defensive over-performance does **not** travel when a coach changes schools — defensive over-perf is a *program* trait (across-coach r=0.46) not a coach one (coach-travel r=0.08), and the O/D *specialization* (the only net-new thing the split surfaces) is **noise** at the coach level (travel r=−0.01, disattenuated −0.03, confirmed with sufficient movement: 145 movers). Conceptually: coaching signal lives in the net *level* (general competence raising efficiency), which ADDS across O+D; the O−D *difference* cancels the shared signal and keeps the noise → the split is exactly the wrong axis. So CAE-O/CAE-D would mint a "coaching" grade that misattributes program identity to coaches (the PR-B/PR-E trap). **Salvage:** a side-experiment found that predicting the CamPom *formula primitives* (`ogbpm/dgbpm/usg/min/gp`) and re-deriving the o/d split beats predicting `cam_o/cam_d` directly by 8–32% (`validation/exp_campom_od_architecture.py`) — a real *projection-pipeline* technique looking for an application, NOT a coach metric. **Original design text kept for the record below.** ~~decompose the single coach-above-expectation grade into an offensive and a defensive half so the board can tell an offensive system-builder (Few) from a defensive one (Bennett)~~: `CAE_O = actual_AdjO − projected_AdjO` and `CAE_D = projected_AdjD − actual_AdjD` (sign-flipped so **+ = better defense**), which **sum to the overall CAE** because `AdjEM = AdjO − AdjD` — a clean, legible decomposition ("Coach X: +3 offense, +1 defense → +4 CAE"). **What we already have**: actual team AdjO/AdjD (`team_season_stats`, now displayed on the coach pages) **and offensive/defensive CamPom splits** (`cam_o_gbpm_v3`, `cam_d_gbpm_v3` + v1/v2/psos variants — **so no new CamPom is needed**, contrary to the first instinct; the o/d composites already exist). **What's missing — the load-bearing gap**: *projected* AdjO and *projected* AdjD. The roster projection (`cstat_core::roster_projection::score_projection_adj_em`) outputs only a single net AdjEM triple `(floor, ceiling, midpoint)` — there is **no O/D split**. So this needs **two new roster-projection models** (projected AdjO, projected AdjD) trained on offensive- and defensive-leaning roster features built from the existing CamPom o/d composites, each with its own PIT LOSO backtest mirroring #96 — the same accuracy discipline as the net projection. That model work is the bulk of the effort; the surfacing is cheap once the projections exist. **Key design decision**: **(a)** *replace* the net AdjEM projection with `proj_AdjO − proj_AdjD` so the headline and the splits reconcile exactly — cleaner, but it changes the served `/api/projections` number and must re-clear the net model's accuracy bar (risky; treat as a separately-gated change); or **(b)** keep the net AdjEM model as the headline and add the O/D projections as a *parallel descriptive decomposition* that approximately sums (`CAE_O + CAE_D ≈ CAE`, small residual) — safer, never touches the served forecast. **Recommend (b) for v1** (descriptive, decoupled from the live projection), with (a) as a possible later unification once the O/D models prove out. **Leakage wall unchanged**: actual AdjO/AdjD are projection *targets* — display-only, never fed back into any forecast; the O/D projections inherit the same no-leakage / PIT discipline as overall CAE ([[project_projection_q1_bias_refuted]]). **Compute**: extend `training/compute_cae.py` with an O/D mode once the O/D projection dump exists (it already de-biases by projection quartile — do the same per split). **Surfaces**: CAE-O / CAE-D columns on the `/coaches` board (sortable, alongside the overall CAE) + a two-bar offensive/defensive split on coach detail. **Effort**: multi-PR, design-doc-worthy — gated entirely on the two O/D roster-projection models + backtests. **Out of scope for the display-only team-strength PR** (which ships *actual* AdjO/AdjD only — the descriptive half; the *projected* half is this item).
- [ ] **Projection-coverage gap on heavy-rebuild rosters — "coached, but unprojectable"** *(surfaced 2026-06-02 while debugging the coach board)* — a coach can coach a season we have the **actual** result for, yet that season gets **no CAE grade** because we couldn't build a roster *projection* for it (CAE = `actual − projection`, so a missing projection means nothing to grade against). The roster projection drops any team-season with fewer than `MIN_QUALIFYING_FOR_PROJECTION = 7` *valuable* players (`roster_projection.rs`) — and a coach who rebuilds almost entirely through the portal trips that gate in year one. **Concrete cases**: **Eric Olen — New Mexico 2026** (left UC San Diego; NM's 2026 roster is 12 players but only ~4 returners with prior-D-I stats, the rest his transfer haul → no projection → unscored, so his CAE still spans only his scored UCSD 2024–25) and **Ben McCollum — Drake 2025** (arrived from D-II Northwest Missouri State with a transfer-heavy roster → same drop; his only scored season is Iowa 2026). This isn't rare: **only 285 of 364 D-I teams were scored in 2026** (~79 dropped, skewed toward new-coach / heavy-portal rosters). **Today's mitigation (shipped)**: the leaderboard's Tenure column reads `coach_seasons` not the scored rating, so these coaches still show **active at their current school** (`Olen · New Mexico · 2024–active`) — the *display* is right; only the *grade* for the unscored season is absent (and the detail page honestly shows just the scored seasons). **Real fix (projection-engine work, multi-PR)**: make heavy-rebuild rosters projectable — ~~(a) resolve incoming transfers by `torvik_pid`, not `natstat_id`~~ **(a) REFUTED 2026-06-02 — torvik_pid is a no-op for the projection.** Traced `compose_all_projections`: incoming arrivals are keyed on `transfers.cstat_player_id`, which the ingest resolver (`resolve_cstat_joins`) fills by **name + source-school match** against the base-season roster — it *never* uses `natstat_id`, so it's already immune to the id-reissue bug. The 25.7%-drop figure ([[feedback_cross_season_player_joins]]) was a *Python cross-season returning-player traversal* bug, already fixed — a different join than anything in the Rust projection. Verified vs the canonical case: **New Mexico 2026 dropped because all 8 qualifying 2025 NM players graduated/transferred out (returning = 0)**, not because any transfer failed to resolve — all 5 transfers in resolved fine (4 qualifying); 0 returning + 4 arrivals + 2 recruits = 6 < 7. torvik_pid also *can't* recover the 218 unresolved 2025 transfers: the 247 feed carries names, so reaching a torvik_pid still requires a name match. The real levers are (b)/(c). **(b)** value D-II→D-I and other no-prior-D-I newcomers from recruit/portal data instead of excluding them; **(c)** consider relaxing the qualifying gate with extra shrinkage rather than a hard drop, so a thin-but-known roster gets a wide-band projection instead of none. **Caveat on (c) alone**: without (b), a relaxed gate projects heavy-rebuild teams off their *incomplete* known roster → systematically lowballs exactly the portal-built successes (Ole Miss +26.2, Drake +18.1 were both dropped in 2025) → CAE = actual − projection mints a *fake overachievement* grade (the PR-B outcome-conditioned trap, [[project_projection_q1_bias_refuted]]). So (c) needs (b) first, or it's worse than honest absence — which is why thin teams stay out of the projection/CAE for now. **Honesty surface (SHIPPED 2026-06-02)**: `queries::get_coach_seasons` now drives off `coach_seasons` (all coached seasons) LEFT JOIN `coach_season_cae`, so the coach-detail page lists *every* coached season — ungraded ones (Olen NM 2026 actual +19.8, McCollum Drake 2025 +18.1) render as team entries marked "not scored" with their actual AdjEM/AdjO/AdjD shown; header reads "X of Y seasons scored"; sparkline plots graded seasons only. No projection-engine / CAE change. **Bounded by**: making teams projectable (b/c) would widen CAE coverage generally, compounding with the "extend the backtest pre-2022" lever. Design-doc-worthy on the projection side.
- [ ] **PBP-derived features in models and UI** — depends on the `play_by_play` table landing (see Refactor Backlog "Play-by-play ingestion"; **full design in `docs/pbp_methodology.md`**). Once PBP is loaded, several model and UI surfaces unlock that we currently have *no path to* from box-score data alone. Order of work: ingest first (CSV + API) ✓, derive per-player aggregates ✓ (P2a), derive raw stints/lineups ✓ (P2b), possession & tempo-normalize ✓ (P3, shipped 2026-06-07), Tier-1 tag features into DB/UI ✓ + models ✗ (backtested and rejected 2026-06-10, see the Tier-1 plan below). **→ Tier-2 source lock LANDED (2026-06-10/11): de-risk PASSED, the durable `cstat-ingest lineups` capture path shipped (migration 037), the 12-season backfill is running, and the compute-side source swap shipped — coherence-gated per team-game** (the object turned out era-incomplete: see the coverage-reality bullet under P-onfloor-4). **→ Membership-features backtest pass (2026-06-11): team-level `lineup_quality` → game models REJECTED (all 7 holdout metrics worse); player-level prior-season on/off → trajectory POSITIVE (covered −0.011 MAE, 9/11 LOPO pairs, every class bucket improves — the first positive PBP-feature verdict)** — see the verdicts bullet under P-onfloor-4. **→ Trajectory on/off features ACCEPTED + SHIPPED 2026-06-11** — the 48→51 contract change landed end-to-end (production retrain pooled LOPO 2.133 → 2.127, `prior_on_net_rtg` 3rd by importance in the final fit; ONNX re-export + Rust `TRAJECTORY_FEATURE_NAMES` + `player_on_off` serving lookup with −999 sentinel; regenerated OOF → roster-impact retrain → 11-season backtest dump → CAE — details in the ship bullet under P-onfloor-4). **→ RAPM COMPLETE 2026-06-12: rejected as a value metric, SHIPPED narrowed as "Adj on/off (RAPM)"** — the display-only companion line in the on/off panel (zero-prior fit, `player_rapm` × 11 seasons, `replay_shadow` corpus protection); the trajectory-slot test also resolved (swap −0.010 decisively worse, add +0.0009 noise — raw on/off keeps its contract slot). Full record: `docs/rapm_methodology.md` §8, and the RAPM item below.
  - **New compute step: `compute_pbp_aggregates`** (runs after `backfill_game_stats`, before `compute_player_season_stats`). Derives per `(player_id, game_id)`:
    - `plus_minus_pbp` — sum of stint score deltas across the player's on-floor stints (lineups from **SUB-replay**, not `onfloorhome` — that's API-only; see methodology doc). Overwrites the sparse box-score `plus_minus` field
    - `seconds_on_court` — precise minutes-on-floor (replaces or sanity-checks NatStat's `MIN`)
    - `paint_fga`, `paint_fgm`, `perimeter_fga`, `perimeter_fgm` — from `tags LIKE '%paint%'`
    - `transition_points`, `second_chance_points`, `points_off_turnovers` — from `brk|`, `2ch|`, `offto|` tags
    - `fouls_drawn` — distinct `FOULED|` event count for player_id
    - `assists_with_recipients` JSONB — per-game `{recipient_player_id: count}` map for assist-network analysis
  - **New tables**:
    - `lineup_stints (game_id, period, start_clock, end_clock, home_lineup INT[5], vis_lineup INT[5], home_score_delta, vis_score_delta, possessions)` — one row per unique 5-man on-floor combination's contiguous time window. Derived by `GROUP BY (game_id, onfloorhome, onfloorvis)` with clock boundaries.
    - `lineup_aggregates (season, team_id, lineup INT[5], minutes, plus_minus, ortg, drtg, possessions)` — season-level rollup of `lineup_stints` for the team-detail "top 5-man lineups" surface.
    - Optional: `assist_edges (season, passer_id, scorer_id, count)` for the assist-network graph.
  - **ML model features** (margin / win prediction, `training/features.py`):
    - Team-level: `lineup_quality` (minutes-weighted +/- per 100 poss of top 5 lineups), `transition_rate` (transition pts / total pts), `second_chance_rate`, `paint_rate` (paint FGA / FGA)
    - Star-player: top-3 GBPM players' lineup-quality average (catches "is this team's best lineup the one that plays the most?")
    - Risk: PBP features only available from 2013 onward (NatStat's PBP coverage start). Either train two model variants (PBP-era vs pre-PBP) or impute missing features with season averages. Document the decision in `docs/features_methodology.md`.
  - **Trajectory + freshman models**:
    - Add `prior_lineup_plus_minus` as a quality-of-fit signal (does this player play in good lineups? predicts whether their counting stats are inflated or suppressed)
    - Add `prior_paint_rate` as a position-proxy refinement (paint% > 60% with low height = inside-out big who'll grow into 4)
  - **Data coverage (updated 2026-06-07)**: the historical PBP backfill has **landed across all 12 seasons (2015–2026) on prod**. Both *aggregated* serving surfaces now render for every season: `lineup_aggregates` (639,980 rows, 2015–2026) backs the team top-5 lineups, and the `player_game_stats` PBP columns (`plus_minus_pbp`, paint/perimeter, transition/2nd-chance/off-TO, fouls drawn — non-null for all 12 seasons) back the player PBP profile. Prod and local match row-for-row on both. All currently-loaded lineups are `replay`-sourced (~86%); the exact `onfloor` path only populates for API-ingested games going forward. **Still local-only by design** (excluded from `sync_to_prod.sh`, 0 rows on prod): raw `play_by_play` (32.8M rows local) and `lineup_stints` (5.1M rows local) — too large to ship, and only needed for ML training and the future game-detail timeline, not the live aggregated surfaces. This is why the on/off-splits, lineup-partners, and game-timeline items below remain blocked: they read per-stint membership from `lineup_stints`, which is not on prod.
  - **P3 (SHIPPED 2026-06-07): possession & tempo normalization (prerequisite for A/B/C below)** *(added 2026-06-07)*: the shipped stint/aggregate derivations are **raw-count only** — `lineup_stints` carries `points_for`/`points_against` + `start_seq`/`end_seq` (no possessions, no duration); `lineup_aggregates` carries `points_for`/`points_against`/`plus_minus`/`stint_count` (no possessions, no minutes, no ortg/drtg). So the served "+/-" has **no denominator**: a 200-possession lineup and a 5-possession lineup sum into the same +/-, conflating quality with floor-time. The roadmap's *planned* `lineup_aggregates` schema (`minutes, ortg, drtg, possessions`) and the ML `lineup_quality` spec ("+/- **per 100 poss**") both assume a tempo-free unit that was never built. **Build this first** — every downstream surface needs it:
    - **Unit of record: possessions** (not "plays" / not minutes). Tempo-free, matches the rest of cstat (four factors, AdjO/AdjD are all per-100-possession). "Plays" (splitting offensive rebounds into a new play) is a defensible alternative but adds methodology surface for no consistency win; minutes is wall-clock, not opportunity-normalized.
    - **Derivation** — from `play_by_play`, exact event-based possession counting (a possession ends on a made FG that isn't an and-1, a defensive rebound, a turnover, or a final made FT). Tags + `scoring_play`/`points` carry the events; `score_diff` flips confirm change-of-possession. Add stint **duration** in parallel by parsing `clock` (TEXT `mm:ss`) at `start_seq`/`end_seq` — cheaper, and needed for a minutes sanity-check and per-40 surfaces. Cross-check the per-stint possession sum against the box-score estimate (`FGA − ORB + TOV + 0.475·FTA`) at the team-game level as a parity gate (same discipline as the TreeSHAP/CamPom train-serve checks).
    - **Schema** — add `possessions_for`, `possessions_against`, `seconds` to `lineup_stints`; add `possessions_for`, `possessions_against`, `minutes`, `ortg` (100·PF/poss_for), `drtg` (100·PA/poss_against), `net_rtg` to `lineup_aggregates` (new migration — never edit `031`). Recompute in `compute_pbp_lineups`; all 12 seasons re-derive locally off the 5.1M-row `lineup_stints` already on disk (no API budget). `lineup_aggregates` is the prod-resident surface, so this also upgrades the **already-shipped** team top-5 lineups UI from raw +/- to per-100 ortg/drtg for free.
    - **Then** the team-lineups surface shows rate stats, the `lineup_quality` ML feature (B) becomes well-defined, and the on/off rollup (A) is computed in the same possession-aware pass. Possession-normalization is the common dependency — do it once here, not three times downstream.
    - **Possession formula = `FGA − ORB + TOV + 0.44·FTA`** — the exact convention cstat already uses for tempo / AdjO / AdjD (`compute.rs::compute_adjusted_efficiency`, the **0.44** FTA coefficient, not 0.475). Per-stint components come from `play_by_play.tags` (`FGA`/`ORB`/`TO`/`FTA`) attributed to the acting `team_id` within `[start_seq, end_seq]`; `possessions_for` uses the stint team's events, `possessions_against` the opponent's. Reconciling to the same formula keeps lineup ortg/drtg on the same scale as team AdjO/AdjD.
    - **P3 implementation — SHIPPED 2026-06-07**:
      - [x] Migration `033_pbp_possessions` — `possessions_for`/`possessions_against`/`seconds` on `lineup_stints`; `possessions_for`/`possessions_against`/`minutes`/`ortg`/`drtg`/`net_rtg` on `lineup_aggregates` (new file; `031` untouched). Possessions stored `DOUBLE PRECISION` to keep the fractional 0.44·FTA term.
      - [x] Engine (`pbp_replay.rs::stint_metrics`): single merge-walk over the seq-ordered plays vs the contiguous stints, tallying per-side possessions `(FGA + 3FA) − ORB + TOV + 0.44·FTA` and on-floor seconds (positive clock decrements; period resets self-cancel). **Three feed-vintage gotchas, all caught by the parity/minutes checks and now covered by unit tests**: (1) the `FGA` tag is **2-point attempts only** — `3FA` is mutually exclusive, so total FGA = `FGA + 3FA`; (2) the **turnover tag changed vintage** — `TOV` in the 2015-2018 feeds, `TO` from ~2019 on (2019 mixes both, non-overlapping), so the count must be `TO || TOV` (omitting legacy `TOV` undercounted 2015-2018 by ~13 turnovers/team-game, 26% MAE → 4-5%); (3) the **clock delimiter drifts** — `MM:SS` (2015-2018), `MM:SS:hh` colon-hundredths (2019-2024), `MM:SS.hh` dot-hundredths (2025-2026) — so `parse_clock` splits on both `:` and `.` and takes the first two fields as minutes/seconds (no college clock exceeds 20:00, so field 1 is always minutes). Handling only two forms had silently zeroed `minutes` for 2020-2024. `clock` added to `RawPlay` + the `load_raw_plays` SELECT. Pure unit tests for `parse_clock` (all three formats), possession counting, stint-window attribution, and the legacy-`TOV` path.
      - [x] Runner (`compute_pbp_lineups`): writes the new stint columns; the season rollup now emits per-100 `ortg`/`drtg`/`net_rtg` + `minutes` (NULL-guarded division). Re-derives all 12 seasons locally off the on-disk `lineup_stints` (no API budget) — backfill run 2026-06-07.
      - [x] Parity test (`tests/pbp_possession_parity.rs`, `#[ignore]`-gated like the oracle test): per-team-game stint possession sum vs the box-score estimate. **2026: MAE 3.0 poss/team-game (4.3%), mean err −2.9** (slight undercount from off-five drift + NULL-team marker rows — expected). Tolerance gate at 8%.
      - [x] In-season freshness: `compute_pbp_lineups` runs inside `compute_all`, which `season` / `update` / `compute` all call — no extra wiring needed.
      - [x] **Freebie delivered**: `GET /api/teams/:id/lineups` (`TeamLineup`) now returns `possessions_for/against`, `minutes`, `ortg`/`drtg`/`net_rtg`. The TeamDetail Top-5 Lineups waffle is now **ranked by minutes (most-used first)** and shows **minutes · off/def rating per 100 poss · net/100** — all tempo-free. **Design note**: an earlier pass showed raw points-for/against, but that re-introduces the exact volume confound P3 removes (a great defensive lineup that plays 400 min racks up more total points-against than a bad one that played 20), so every displayed stat is per-100. Ranking by minutes (not stint count) because a lineup can log many short stints or a few long ones — minutes is the true "most-used" measure.
      - [x] **Clock-vintage / minutes fix**: `parse_clock` now handles all three delimiter vintages (see engine bullet); 2020-2024 re-derived so `minutes` populates for every non-gated season (~5-6 min/lineup avg). Ranking by minutes depends on this.
      - [x] **Before this flows to prod**: `lineup_aggregates` ships via `sync_to_prod.sh`, so run `./scripts/sync_to_prod.sh` after the local backfill to push the new rate columns and the cleared 2019 state (`lineup_stints` stays local — its columns are inputs only). **Synced 2026-06-10.**
    - **SUB-replay lineup drift inflates minutes/possessions (data-quality finding, surfaced 2026-06-07 spot-checking Duke 2025)**: the served lineups are SUB-reconstructed for **every** season (onfloor coverage is 0 right now), and the replay drifts enough to surface phantom top lineups. Concrete case: Duke 2025's *second-most-used* lineup (Foster/Evans/Gillis/Flagg/Brown) showed **121 min** — but four of those five are ~14-16 MPG bench players, and per-game the lineup's claimed minutes **exceed the least-playing member's box minutes** (e.g. vs Georgia Tech it claimed 10.4 min while Gillis played only 8 all game). Root cause: **27% of Duke 2025 SUB plays carry a NULL `player_id`** (unresolved even after the name fallback), so a missed sub-out leaves a player "on" past their exit — stretching the stint window, which both invents the 5-man combo and over-counts its minutes *and* possessions (possessions are counted within the same too-long window, so per-100 rates are computed on a wrong denominator too). Magnitude: **17% of Duke 2025 lineup-game rows are physically impossible** (claimed > min box minutes of members), ~313 phantom minutes. This is the methodology doc's ~86% replay accuracy biting the served surface. **Options** (not yet decided): (a) **sanity-clamp/exclude** lineup-game contributions that exceed the box-minute ceiling — cheap hygiene, removes the egregious artifacts but not all drift; (b) **API-native onfloor lineups** (the discovered `lineups` hydration + per-play `onfloorhome`/`onfloorvis`) — the real fix, exact 5-man with no replay, but only populates the in-season API path going forward and has unconfirmed historical coverage; (c) **improve sub resolution** to cut the 27% NULL-player rate; (d) **confidence caveat** — the waffle already tags `replay` source (~86%) in a tooltip, but the ranking still trusts the drift. Likely path: (a) now as hygiene + prioritize (b) for accuracy. **Blocks trusting**: the "most-used lineup" ranking, lineup minutes, and replay-sourced lineup ortg/drtg until mitigated.
      - **Scope across the site (what's contaminated vs clean)** — measured 2026-06-07:
        - **Contaminated (replay-derived, gated on stints):** `lineup_aggregates` (minutes, ortg/drtg/net, ranking) and `player_game_stats.plus_minus_pbp` (summed over 5-man stints). Severity is uneven — *physically-impossible* lineup-game rows run **1-4% for 2015-2024 but 11% for 2025** (and 4% for 2026); a *separate* minutes problem is that per-team lineup-minutes should tile ~40/game but the derivation captures **only ~32-35/game in 2015-2024 (under) and ~43/game in 2025-26 (over)** — box minutes are clean (~197/team-game), so the error is entirely in the stint derivation. Driver: stint length inflates with missed subs (2025 avg 59.7s/stint vs 2024 48.4s; ~half of NULL-player subs are genuinely unresolvable, not a name-match bug).
        - **Clean (NOT replay-dependent):** the per-player PBP aggregates — `paint_fga/fgm`, `perimeter_fga/fgm`, `transition_pts`, `second_chance_pts`, `points_off_turnovers`, `fouls_drawn` — come from `compute_pbp_aggregates`, a **direct tag rollup keyed on `player_id`** (no stints, no replay), so they're as good as the tag stream (i.e. fine outside gated-2019). The team-game possession parity is also fine in aggregate (4-8% MAE); only the *per-lineup* slice carries the drift.
      - **Mitigation — box-minute clamp SHIPPED 2026-06-07**: `compute_pbp_lineups` now materializes a per-(game, team, lineup) temp rollup with a `valid` flag — a (game, lineup) is invalid when its summed minutes exceed `min(box minutes of its five) + 1.0` (tolerance for box-minute integer rounding; COALESCE to a huge ceiling when box minutes are unknown, so we only drop what we can *prove* impossible). Both served surfaces — `lineup_aggregates` and `player_game_stats.plus_minus_pbp` — read only valid game-lineups, so they reconcile. The raw `lineup_stints` is untouched (a serving filter, not a delete), so the parity test (which reads `lineup_stints`) is unaffected. **Result on the canonical case**: Duke 2025's phantom #2 lineup (Foster/Evans/Gillis/Flagg/Brown) dropped from **121 min / 48 stints → 39 min / 13 stints** and fell to #4; the real starter/Brown units are unchanged and rank correctly. This is hygiene, not a cure — it removes *provably-impossible* game-lineups but not subtler membership drift, so the `replay`-source caveat stays on the UI. The real fix is the onfloor ingest below.
    - **2019 PBP tag corruption (data-quality finding, surfaced 2026-06-07 by the P3 parity gate)**: NatStat's **2019** Play-by-Play CSV export is corrupt at the source — made field goals are mis-encoded as free throws. The raw row for "John Petty Jr. made layup" carries `Points=1` + `Tags=FTA|FTM|fbrk` instead of `Points=2` + `FGA|FGM|paint` (the 2020 export tags the identical play correctly). This halves 2019's `FGA`/`FGM`, inflates `FTA`, and runs its derived possessions **~36% low** (vs 1.9-7.8% for every other season). It corrupts **all** 2019 PBP-derived surfaces — possessions/ortg/drtg, paint/perimeter mix, transition/2nd-chance/off-TO points, `plus_minus_pbp`. The **box scores are unaffected** (separate `Player_Statlines` CSV), so non-PBP 2019 data is fine. Not a parser bug — our column indices are right; the source columns hold wrong values. **Resolved 2026-06-07 — suppressed via an auto-detecting coverage gate.** `compute.rs::pbp_source_is_corrupt` measures each season's PBP `FGA`+`3FA` tag coverage vs box FGA (`PBP_MIN_FGA_COVERAGE = 0.80`; 2019 ≈ 0.55, every clean season > 0.93). When a season fails, `compute_pbp_aggregates` **clears and skips** that season's tag-derived per-player surfaces (paint/perimeter mix, transition/2nd-chance/off-TO points). **A clean 2019 re-fetch does not exist**: probed the API directly — `/playbyplay/mbb/2018-11-06` returns `NO_DATA`, and the hydrated single-game endpoint returns lineups but no raw play stream, so there's no alternative PBP source for 2019. **Update 2026-06-14 (#119 dig) — the corruption gate for LINEUPS is now surgical, not clear-and-skip.** The corruption is possession-only: lineup membership (SUB events), minutes (the clock), and points/`plus_minus` (the running `score_home`/`score_vis` field, *not* tag-summed) are all immune. So `compute_pbp_lineups` no longer discards a corrupt season's replay — it keeps the valid membership/minutes/plus-minus and *repairs* the one bad dimension by replacing each served stint's possessions with a clock-based estimate (its share of the team's on-floor seconds × the clean box-possession total per game/team). This recovered Duke 2019's waffle from a single coherent game (9-min top lineup, no Zion) to 111 lineups / 180-min top with valid ratings, and on/off from 1,952 → 3,617 players. Blast radius is 2019 only (every other season's FGA coverage is > 0.93). Guarded by `tests/corrupt_season_lineups.rs`.

Per unit: `lineupplayers`, `possessions`, `points`/`points-d`, `plusminus`, `oppp`/`dppp` (off/def **points per possession** — exact ortg/drtg), full shooting splits with offensive/defensive/margin variants (`fgm`/`fgm-d`/`fgm-m`, `fga`, `threefm`, `threefa`, `ftm`, `fta`, `reb`, `ast`, `blk`, `stl`, `tov`, `pf`), and **play-style points**: `ptspaint`, `ptsbrk` (transition), `pts2ch` (second-chance), `ptsoffto` — each with a `-d` defensive counterpart. This is a full lineup four-factor + play-style profile **including the defensive side**, which our replay-`plus_minus` can't isolate well.
    - **`playerperfs`** — per-game player box already carrying derived context we currently recompute: `usgpct`, `teamposs`, `teamfga`/`teamfta`/`teamto`, `presencerate`/`adjpresencerate` (NatStat's on-court presence ≈ minute share), `eff`, `perfscore`/`perfscoreseasonavg`/`...dev`.

Likely play: keep replay as the historical/bulk backbone, layer API-native lineups for the in-season path (which already calls the API) and as an accuracy oracle. Coverage for older seasons is unconfirmed (2019 hydrate returned lineups but they likely inherit the same corrupt PBP source — verify before trusting). **Full incorporation brainstorm in the "Possession & lineup data — feature incorporation" item below.**

The box-minute clamp (shipped) only removes *provably-impossible* phantoms; this kills the drift at the source and adds defensive lineup ratings the replay structurally can't produce. **Scope / phasing**:
    - **P-onfloor-1 — populate `play_by_play.onfloor_home`/`onfloor_vis`** (columns already exist, migration `031`, currently NULL for every row since all PBP is CSV-loaded). The API play objects carry `game.onfloorhome`/`onfloorvis` (comma-separated NatStat player codes); `parse_api_play` (`playbyplay.rs`) already reads them into `PbpRow.onfloor_*` — so the **in-season API path already stores onfloor for games it ingests**. Nothing to build for *new* in-season games; the gap is historical backfill.
